| P2.0a3 | DONE | P2.0a2, P2.0d2 | Circuit timing as C# does it (18 November 2026, from the review of #25 to #37 at `ebda8a9`). The findings were checked by the reviewers and the top ones by the manager; confirm each against the code and the C# at the pin first, and record here any that turn out to be wrong instead of fixing them. (1) Resends: Ferroan resends after 200 ms, four times, then drops the packet (`crates/ferroan-client/src/circuit.rs` `RESEND_AFTER`, `MAX_RESENDS`). Tranquillity starts the retransmission timeout at 1000 ms, adjusts it from ping round trips between its limits, and never drops an unacked packet; it keeps resending until the client is timed out (`LLUDPClient.cs` `m_RTO`, `m_defaultRTO`, `m_maxRTO`, `UpdateRoundTrip`; `LLUDPServer.cs` `HandleUnacked`, `ResendUnacked`). A loss burst of about a second during login loses `RegionHandshake` or `AgentMovementComplete` for good, and a viewer with more than 200 ms of latency gets every reliable packet twice. Port C#'s timer and retry behavior, bounded by the inactivity timeout. (2) Inactivity: the circuit ends only on logout or a fixed 300 s lifetime (`ferroan-region/src/main.rs`). Track the last packet received and end the circuit after C#'s `AckTimeout` of silence (`LLUDPServer.cs` `DeactivateClientDueToTimeout`), and drop the fixed lifetime. (3) Re-login: a `CreateAgent` for an agent that already has a circuit is refused until the old one expires. C# removes the stale presence and accepts the new login (`Scene.cs` `NewUserConnection`); do the same. (4) Logout tail: C# sends `KickUser` and `KillObject` after `LogoutReply` (see `fixtures/circuit/manifest.json`); the generator filters them out and Ferroan closes after `LogoutReply`. Port `KickUser` and record `KillObject` as waiting for P2.0f, or record why neither is sent. (5) `ReliableWindow::oldest_or` takes the smallest sequence number as the oldest; use send order as `UnackedPacketCollection` does. (6) The established-ACK and logout tests seed Ferroan's sequence counter from the capture; drive them from a captured inbound sequence, as the arrival test does. Acceptance: unit tests with a fake clock show the C# resend schedule, RTO adjustment from ping, no drop before the inactivity timeout, and the timeout itself; a second login for the same agent replaces the first; `cargo xtask live run login grid --region ferroan` passes; and the bot's loss tests pass with a one-second loss burst during login. **Evidence:** [PR #45](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/pull/45), implementation `f264f04`. Findings 1–3 and 6 confirmed and fixed against Tranquillity `858321883f5487a5d192d36ea9d7b90fa468310c`: `LLUDPClient`/`LLUDPServer` timers and ping filtering, `LLClientView` pause/resume, and `Scene.NewUserConnection` stale-session replacement. Finding 4 needs qualification: the fresh 55-datagram C# capture contains `KickUser` with "The simulator is going down.", emitted by `Scene.Close`, not ordinary `CloseAgent`/`CloseWithoutChecks`; no ordinary-logout kick was added. `KillObject` remains deferred to P2.0f; it is absent from this fresh capture. Finding 5 is false at the pin: `UnackedPacketCollection` uses `SortedDictionary<uint, OutgoingPacket>` and selects the numeric minimum; existing ordering is retained and its wrap test records that source. `initial_resend_schedule_retains_unacked_until_strict_inactivity_deadline`, ping-filter/override/pause/resume/active-beyond-five-minutes tests use a fake clock; both `bot_login_survives_one_second_*_loss` tests pass through a real UDP relay (and failed with the old resend cutoff). Region HTTP tests prove same-agent replacement, cancellation and generation-safe seed cleanup. ACK/logout tests drive the captured incoming trace without counter or ACK-queue seeding; only four sequence bytes differ because unimplemented C# world sends advance its counter, and Rust's own sequence allocation is asserted. `cargo xtask check` passes; C# `live run login grid`: 55 UDP / 2 HTTP, capture check/redact/replay pass; Rust `live run login grid --region ferroan`: login and LogoutReply pass, registration/QueryAccess/CreateAgent/deregistration match C#, HTTP robust-private 10 / robust-public 0 / home 4 / neighbour 0. D19 documents rejected invalid timer configuration and ignored unsolicited pre-ping RTT samples. [CI](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44427874954): Linux 2m30s, Windows 3m49s, macOS 4m11s; [live](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44427874884) 9m31s, all green. Live report: standalone 745 UDP / 32 HTTP; grid 894 UDP / 34 HTTP; capture check/redact/replay pass; Rust login and LogoutReply pass, grid-login HTTP matches C#; held empty polls 58055/58003 ms, assets match 12 HTTP records. Firestorm remains pending; the production dispatcher has not been exercised by two consecutive live logins here. |
| P2.0a4 | DONE | P2.0a3 | Circuit close and validation as C# does it (added 19 November 2026, from the manager's review of #40 to #48 at `1076a0a`; verify each finding against the C# at the pin before fixing it, and record any you reject with the reason). (1) Timeout and re-login close the old circuit silently; C# `CloseAgent(id, true)` sends a reliable `DisableSimulator` to the old endpoint (`Scene.cs:4308`, `LLUDPServer.cs:1815`, `LLClientView.cs:572-613`). (2) Region shutdown sends nothing; C# `Scene.Close` sends `KickUser` "The simulator is going down." then `DisableSimulator` (`Scene.cs:1572-1586`); the captured `fixtures/circuit/kick-user.bin` covers the kick. (3) While a replaced circuit is still being served, a new `UseCircuitCode` from another endpoint gets no ACK (`circuit.rs` returns early when `peer != from`); C# always `SendAckImmediate`s it (`LLUDPServer.cs:1266-1283`). (4) `StartPingCheck.OldestUnacked` sends 0 once nothing is pending (`oldest_or(0)`); C# keeps the last oldest (`UnackedPacketCollection.cs`). (5) The inactivity clock starts when serving starts, so a viewer slower than 60 s to send `UseCircuitCode` is dropped and reported logged out; C# starts it at `LLUDPClient` creation (`LLUDPClient.cs:241`). (6) Agent and session IDs are checked on 7 message types; C# checks every type libopenmetaverse marks `NeedValidateIDs` and drops a mismatch before ACK processing (`LLUDPServer.cs:1327-1330`). (7) From #40: the encoder omits a trailing block it was told to omit even when it holds data (`crates/ferroan-protocol/build.rs`, `ImprovedInstantMessage::default()` sets one omitted block); omit only empty blocks. (8) From #40: an inbound packet with a partly present trailing Single block is refused, where libopenmetaverse `FromBytes` ignores bytes past its template; match it or record a decision. (9) From #40: C# `SendInstantMessage` sends the IM without zerocoding (`LLClientView.cs:1115-1119`) although the template marks it zerocoded; note the per-sender flag in PROTOCOL.md for the future IM sender. (10) The loss tests drop only one server-to-client message; also drop the viewer's ACKs and a whole one-second burst both ways. Acceptance: captured C# `DisableSimulator` and `KickUser` sends, and tests showing (1) to (6); `live run login grid --region ferroan` passes; `cargo xtask check` passes. **Evidence:** [PR #91](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/pull/91), implementation `0c4a8e6`. Verified against Tranquillity `40432e0` and libopenmetaverse `a3596616`. No finding rejected. (1)(2) Timeout and re-login send reliable `DisableSimulator` only when a peer is mapped. Region shutdown sends reliable `KickUser` (target IP 0, port 0, reason `The simulator is going down.`) then `DisableSimulator`. Ordinary logout sends neither. No 500 ms flush sleep. `fixtures/circuit/shutdown-kick-8583218.bin` is unchanged. `fixtures/circuit/disable-simulator.bin` is the 10-byte constructor datagram `400000000000ffff0098` (sequence 0; the live send stamps the sequence). The circuit manifest revision was not changed. (3) A `UseCircuitCode` from another endpoint is ACKed to that endpoint and kept for the next serve. The current peer does not move. (4) `OldestUnacked` updates on every non-empty resend scan and on ping, and a later ping keeps that value after the window empties. (5) Qualified, not rejected: `serve_login` already withheld ack timeout until `connection_opened`; `serve_one` started it at serve start. Timeout now requires a mapped peer. `LoginConnectWait` remains the pre-connect deadline. (6) Generated `id_check` matches every `AgentData` block that contains both `AgentID` and `SessionID`: 232 messages. libopenmetaverse generates 229 Packet classes with `NeedValidateIDs`. `AvatarPickerRequestBackend`, `UpdateAttachment` and `RemoveAttachment` match the rule and have no generated class, so Ferroan checks them too. A valid packet still passes. A body too short for the ids is dropped. `last_received` still updates first. (7) Only an empty requested trailing suffix is omitted. (8) A short trailing Single is ignored, matching libopenmetaverse `FromBytes`; a partial Variable block is still an error. `Reader::take` is unchanged. (9) `docs/PROTOCOL.md` says a future region IM sender must pass `zerocoded: false`. No IM sender was added. (10) Loss tests still drop `RegionHandshake` and `AgentMovementComplete` for one second, and also drop viewer acks and a one-second blackout both ways. Login still completes from the real resend. `cargo xtask check` passed locally. The live acceptance run is this PR's live job. Firestorm, and MySQL/PostgreSQL of `--rust assets`, remain pending. |
| P2.0d3 | DONE | P2.0d2, P2.0a3 | Region login: presence, trust and C# parity (18 November 2026, from the review of #25 to #37 at `ebda8a9`). The findings were checked by the reviewers and the top ones by the manager; confirm each against the code and the C# at the pin first, and record here any that turn out to be wrong instead of fixing them. (1) Logout is never reported: when a circuit ends, call GridUser `LoggedOut` and Presence `LogoutAgent` as `ActivityDetector.cs` and `PresenceDetector.cs` do. Without it Robust keeps the user online, friends see them online, and with `AllowDuplicatePresences = false` a later login can be refused (`LLLoginService.cs`). (2) `CreateAgent` trusts any loopback caller: C# also calls `VerifyUserPresence` (`Scene.cs` `NewUserConnection`), asking the Presence service that the session belongs to the agent. Do the same before accepting. (3) Port `ControlPlaneAccess` (`ControlPlaneTrustedHosts`, `TrustedControlPlaneHosts`, IPv4-mapped loopback through `NormalizeAddress`) in place of the hard-coded loopback check; P0.11's containers need it. (4) Clamp the login start position X and Y into the region as `NewUserConnection` does. (5) Semantic `CreateAgent` refusals (destination mismatch, child agent, teleport flags without ViaLogin) answer 400 where C# answers 200 with a reason, which makes the C# connector retry; answer as C# does, and make `QueryAccess` refuse what `CreateAgent` will refuse. (6) Read every `*.ini` in `regionload_regionsdir` as `RegionLoaderFileSystem` does, requiring one region in total for now; refuse `*.xml` with a clear error. (7) `[Startup]` overrides `[Map]` for `GenerateMaptiles` and `MaptileStaticUUID` (`GetConfigVarFromSections`); Ferroan has it the other way. (8) Format `QueryAccess` version numbers as C#'s `Convert.ToString(double)` does, and ignore a non-array `features` value and accept numeric strings as C# does. (9) Refuse to start when `AuthType` is not `None`, or implement Basic auth as `ServiceAuth` does. Tests: (10) build the arrival from `RegionConfig` plus the captured `CreateAgent`, and assert that it differs from the C# capture only in the documented Z (until P2.0e) and channel string; narrow the P2.0d2 evidence to say what the existing test proves. (11) Send the captured request's `application/json` with `X-Content-Encoding: gzip` in the `CreateAgent` test. Acceptance: after `live run login grid --region ferroan` and logout, Robust's GridUser and Presence show the user offline, and a second login succeeds; a `CreateAgent` with a session Presence does not know is refused as C# refuses it; fixtures from the C# region back each changed answer; `cargo xtask check` passes. **Evidence:** [PR #46](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/pull/46), implementation `3173ab0`. Findings 1–4 and 6–11 confirmed against Tranquillity `858321883f5487a5d192d36ea9d7b90fa468310c` and fixed. Finding 5 needs qualification: C# supports child agents and non-login transfers; these remain explicit Ferroan capability limits, using C#'s HTTP 200 refusal envelope (which omits `success` on failure), not fabricated C# refusal reasons. QueryAccess now refuses unsupported crossing/capacity cases. `captured_refusals_and_query_variants_match_csharp_http` checks six fresh C# responses, including unknown Presence session, destination mismatch, widened 0.3 negotiation, ignored non-array features, no overlap and legacy version. `presence_and_logout_forms_match_captured_csharp_exchanges` checks four actual Presence/GridUser exchanges. Presence mismatch/error preserves existing admission; generation-safe logout notifies both services, including timeout and shutdown, and preserves replacement state. Region tests cover all INIs, XML refusal, Startup map precedence, AuthType rejection, trust aliases/mapped IPv6/bounded DNS, X/Y clamping and actual application/json plus X-Content-Encoding:gzip. The configured region's captured-CreateAgent UDP arrival differs only in documented Z/channel after identity/time folding; P2.0d2's earlier claim is narrowed to its actual send-path coverage. Local C# login/probes: 55 UDP / 2 HTTP, check/redact/replay pass, six HTTP answers captured. Local Rust login: `logout state: GridUser offline, Presence empty; second login and logout pass`; `region parity: 6 captured HTTP answers match C#`; `logout: LogoutReply`; registration/QueryAccess/CreateAgent/deregistration match C#; HTTP robust-private 25 / robust-public 0 / home 13 / neighbour 0. D20 and TB5 document the C# empty-host trust bug and explicit configuration rejection; no upstream issue submitted. `cargo xtask check` passes after the final URI/status fixes; 35 region library tests, 3 dispatcher tests and 2 probe/generator tests pass. The final live Rust run repeats the offline/second-login/probe results above. [CI](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44433083049): Linux 2m25s, Windows 4m01s, macOS 3m45s; [live](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44433083139) 9m35s, all green. Live report confirms offline state, second login/logout and six C# HTTP answers; Rust HTTP 23/0/13/0 (robust-private/public/home/neighbour), C# standalone 762 UDP/32 HTTP and grid 918 UDP/34 HTTP, capture check/redact/replay pass; empty polls 58055/58002 ms and assets match 12 HTTP records. Initial [CI](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44432654899) failed on four newer-Clippy needless-borrow warnings; `d63934f` removes them, and the superseded live run was canceled on push. Root-arrival ReportAgent/SetLastPosition hooks remain missing and are recorded under Found while working; these checks prove admission and termination, not active location reporting. Firestorm remains pending; Q10/#41 remains blocked and untouched. |

At Pin8 `c401bceecd`, `Scene.LoadWorldMap` (`Source/OpenSim.Region.Framework/Scenes/Scene.cs` 2313–2385) handles a store with no `terrain` row: it copies a stored `bakedterrain` row and stores it as `terrain` (2335–2340), or else builds `[Terrain] InitialTerrain` (default `pinhead-island`, `RegionServerDefaults.ini` 2835; `flat` is `ClearLand` at 21) with `TerrainChannel(String, int, int, int)` (`TerrainChannel.cs` 73–81, `PinHeadIsland` 553–569, `TerrainUtil.SphericalFactor` in `TerrainUtil.cs` 34–40) for the region's size, and stores it with `StoreTerrain` (2353). When no baked row was loaded, it then stores the heightmap as `bakedterrain` too (2380–2384); `TerrainModule.AddRegion` (`Source/OpenSim.Region.CoreModules/World/Terrain/TerrainModule.cs` 236–262) and `UpdateBakedMap` (752–759) keep the same map as the baked copy. Ferroan reads only a stored `terrain` row from SQLite (`crates/ferroan-scene/src/store.rs`, `load_terrain` in `crates/ferroan-region/src/main.rs`) and starts without a map otherwise, so the harness home store (`objects.db`, no `terrain` row) walks at Z 0 (`walk: pass (128.0,128.0,0.0 to 133.8,128.2,2.1)` on #251). Port the generation, the baked-row fallback and the SQLite writes of both rows (`SQLiteSimulationData.cs` `StoreTerrain` 828, `StoreBakedTerrain` 864, `LoadBakedTerrain` 946; the blob is `TerrainData.GetDatabaseBlob` 307, `ToCompressedTerrainSerializationV2DGzip` 629, revision 23, in `Source/OpenSim.Framework/TerrainData.cs`). The write happens at startup, as C# does it, under D39's rule that Ferroan and C# never open the same store at once; P2.9a's persistence switch later covers it too. MySQL and PostgreSQL are P2.0e3. **Evidence:** the generated 256 × 256 map equals the cells of `fixtures/walk/terrain.bin` (the C# default `pinhead-island` row, revision 23) bit for bit; Ferroan's `LayerData` for a store with no `terrain` row matches the burst in `fixtures/walk/walk.cap`; the stored `terrain` and `bakedterrain` rows are revision 23 and decode to the same cells, and `tools/regionstore-oracle` loads them through C#'s `LoadTerrain` and `LoadBakedTerrain` to the same map (the gzip bytes need not match, since the compressor differs; record whether they do); a store with a `terrain` row and no `bakedterrain` row gets the baked row; `cargo xtask live run walk grid --region ferroan` then starts at the C# walk's height (`128.0,128.0,26.0` in `fixtures/walk/README.md`), and any difference left is the stand-in's and is recorded. Size: about a day. **Done:** `crates/ferroan-scene/src/initial.rs` builds the map, `store.rs` (`load_world_map`) reads and writes both rows in one transaction each, and `ferroan-region` reads `[Terrain] InitialTerrain`. `the_island_is_the_csharp_row_bit_for_bit` matches all 65,536 cells of `fixtures/walk/terrain.bin` after C#'s three-decimal rounding; `the_csharp_layer_burst_is_the_generated_island` finds each of the 256 patches of Ferroan's layers once in the 12 `LayerData` packets of `walk.cap` (C# packs several per packet); `store::tests` cover the empty store (both rows, revision 23, C#'s cells, nothing written on the next start), a `terrain` row without a baked row (baked row written), a baked row without a `terrain` row (copied), a missing file (in memory only) and an oversized or NULL cell (refused before it is read, nothing written). `regionstore_terrain_rows_load_in_csharp` (`cargo xtask regionstore verify`) loads Ferroan's rows through the pin's `LoadTerrain` and `LoadBakedTerrain` to the same cells; the gzip bytes differ, 61,701 vs 64,634 bytes, same cells. A damaged row stops startup (D72, TB88). Live, port base 27000: `walk: pass (128.0,128.0,25.8 to 134.0,128.3,26.9)` (was Z 0; C# `128.0,128.0,26.0 to 134.0,128.3,26.9`), and `cargo xtask live compare region` printed `region: match`. The remaining 0.16 m at the start is the login lift, not the terrain: C# lifts by half of `Appearance.AvatarHeight` (`ScenePresence.cs` 1526, 1534–1539), Ferroan by 0.8; outside this item (`docs/found/Q82.md`). |

| P2.0d6 | DONE | P2.0d4 | Region login robustness and C# parity (added 19 November 2026, from the manager's review of #40 to #48 at `1076a0a`; verify each finding against the C# at the pin before fixing it, and record any you reject with the reason). (1) **First, high:** a failed logout notification stops the whole region. `crates/ferroan-region/src/main.rs` `dispatch_circuits` treats a `notify_logout` error as fatal and `serve` then deregisters and exits. C# only logs connector failures (`PresenceDetector.OnConnectionClose`, `ActivityDetector.OnConnectionClose`), and Robust's Presence `logout` answers `Failure` in ordinary use when the session row is already gone: a re-login within the 60 s ack timeout with `AllowDuplicatePresences=false`, or a login Robust abandons after CreateAgent (`LLLoginService.cs` calls `LogoutAgent`). Log and continue as C# does; the test `griduser_failure_still_removes_presence_and_fails_dispatcher` asserts the current behaviour and must change, and the dispatcher mocks must also answer `Failure`. (2) Presence `logoutregion` (`LogoutRegionAgents(regionID)`) at region start and stop, as `PresenceDetector.AddRegion`/`RemoveRegion` do; since #48 Ferroan's RegionID is stamped on Presence rows. (3) CreateAgent can exceed Robust's 30 s `SimulationServiceConnector.CreateAgent` timeout (15 s lifecycle-lock wait plus a 30 s Presence verify, `simulation.rs`, `grid.rs`); bound the total below it so Robust never times out and retries a login the region then accepts. (4) Replacing a root login: C# `NewUserConnection` closes the old root (firing GridUser `LoggedOut`) before `VerifyUserPresence` (`Scene.cs:4291-4336`); Rust keeps the old circuit when the verify fails and skips that `LoggedOut`. Match C#, or record the difference as a decision and a TB row. (5) Check `LoginsEnabled`/admission before the Presence verify, as `Scene.cs:4126` does. (6) `AuthType` values C# accepts (`none`, empty, `TrustedGridAuthentication`) must not stop startup (`config.rs`; `ServiceAuth.Create`). (7) The refused-POST 403 must be `Content-Type: application/json` with an empty body, as `AgentHandlers.cs:220-221` sends; capture it. Acceptance: a Presence `Failure` on logout outside shutdown leaves the region serving and the next login succeeding, in a test and in `live run login grid --region ferroan`; `logoutregion` appears in the capture at start and stop with the C# form fields; a CreateAgent with Presence delayed finishes inside Robust's timeout; fixtures from C# back (5) to (7); `cargo xtask check` passes. **Evidence:** [PR #81](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/pull/81), implementation `261b111`. Checked against Tranquillity `40432e0`. (1) On `main` at `82d6a98`, `dispatch_circuits` already logs a `notify_logout` error and returns `Ok(())`. `presence_logout_failure_keeps_the_dispatcher_and_the_next_login` answers Presence `logout` with `Failure`; the dispatcher stays up and the next admit succeeds. The live capture still compares `presence-logout` with the C# Success fixture, so that Failure is not injected there. Live still shows the next login: `logout state: GridUser offline, Presence empty; second login and logout pass`. (2) `notify_region_presence` sends Presence `logoutregion` at start and stop, logged and continued on failure. `presence_logoutregion_matches_csharp_and_ignores_failure` and `logout_connectors_send_the_source_fields_and_require_success` match `fixtures/pin3/login-robustness.json`. Live prints `presence logoutregion: start and stop match the C# form`. (3) `SimulationState.create_agent_budget` is 25s, under Robust's 30s `CreateAgent` timeout, and is not a config key. `delayed_presence_create_agent_finishes_inside_the_robust_timeout` returns within 5s while Presence sleeps 30s. `exhausted_create_agent_budget_refuses_busy_without_closing_a_root` refuses busy on a zero budget and on a lock held past the budget, without calling Presence or closing the root. (4) The success path stays verify-then-accept (D22). `replacement_waits_for_root_writes_and_old_generation_cannot_report_again` still sees `getagent`, `report`, `setposition`, `getagent`. `failed_presence_on_a_root_replacement_still_logs_the_old_root_out` closes an existing root only when the replacement verify fails, then the next admit succeeds. No TB row: close-before-verify is intentional C# behavior, not a Tranquillity defect. (5) `admission_is_refused_before_presence_verify` matches the oracle reason `Logins to this region are disabled` and refuses an occupied region before any Presence call. (6) `unsupported_auth_is_refused_for_network_and_each_used_service_without_secrets` follows the oracle: `None`, `none`, empty and `TrustedGridAuthentication` start; `BasicHttpAuthentication` does not. Unknown names are still refused. Signing is not implemented. (7) `refused_post_matches_csharp_json_403` matches the oracle 403, `Content-Type: application/json`, empty body, for an untrusted address and for `X-SecondLife-Shard`. `cargo xtask check` passed locally and `cargo xtask pin3 verify` passed. [CI](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44516991701): Linux 2m19s, Windows 4m2s, macOS 3m17s; [live](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/actions/runs/44516992201) 19m4s, all green on `261b111`. Live report: `login: pass`; `logout state: GridUser offline, Presence empty; second login and logout pass`; `logout: LogoutReply`; `grid login http: register, QueryAccess, CreateAgent, deregister match C#`; `root arrival http: Presence report and GridUser setposition match C# (terrain Z pending)`; `presence logoutregion: start and stop match the C# form`. An entity with the viewer's id is queue 0. Another avatar is queue 1 at any distance. An attachment, and every part of the linkset the viewer is sitting on, is queue 2. Any other prim is measured from the avatar position to the linkset absolute position. The distance queues start at 3 and step at about 20, 40, 80, 160, 320, 640, 1280, 2560 and 5120 metres. A prim whose root has no physical actor, or whose actor is not physical, sits one queue lower, and 12 is the last queue. A distance that is not finite overflows the C# queue index and that update is dropped (TB72). Ferroan sends it to queue 12 (D55). When `[InterestManagement] UpdatePrioritizationScheme` is set to anything other than BestAvatarResponsiveness, startup logs a warning. The angular scheme is not computed. With no presence, only the viewer's own id stays off queue 12. The live packet path does not prioritize, so the compare is unchanged. | P2.1c | DONE (this PR) | P2.1a | Throttles. The `AgentThrottle` token buckets per category: `Source/OpenSim.Region.ClientStack.LindenUDP/TokenBucket.cs` (394), `ThrottleRates.cs` (111) and the throttle half of `LLUDPClient.cs` (829) at `1d7abb8337`. Needed before a store with hundreds of prims floods the viewer. Evidence: bucket math against a small C# oracle under `tools/` if the unit cases cannot be taken from the source alone (D13), and a synthetic flood through one circuit that stays within the rates. The live run that loads the 500-prim seeded store with no drops needs objects, so it is part of P2.5a's evidence. About a day. **Q43** (24 November 2026, `feat/q43-throttle`): rechecked at pin `c401bceecd`. `TokenBucket.cs` is 394 lines, `ThrottleRates.cs` is 111 and `LLUDPClient.cs` is 829. `crates/ferroan-client/src/throttle.rs` holds the category buckets. Clients on one socket share one scene bucket. Stock rates are Resend 6625, Land 9125, Wind 1750, Cloud 1750, Task 18500, Texture 18500, Asset 10500 and Total 66750 bytes/s. `client_throttle_max_bps` (default 640000, clamped to 6250..=1000000, a value that is not an integer ignored; `ThrottleRates.cs` lines 82–86) and `scene_throttle_max_bps` (6250000 when `[ClientStack.LindenUDP]` exists, 0 when it does not; 0 or below is no scene limit; a bad value stops startup; `LLUDPServer.cs` lines 414 and 455) are read beside the circuit timing. Adaptive throttles stay off. A child drip does not fall below 500. A requested burst above 7500 becomes 7500. The first deposit adds nothing. C# takes it on the first packet of each category, which is refused once and leaves on the next send pass, because it never asks an empty queue for tokens. Ferroan takes it when the bucket is opened, so that packet leaves one send pass earlier. A send is allowed while any credit remains and that packet may overdraw. `AgentThrottle` is seven little-endian floats, scaled by 0.125, floored (wind and cloud at 50, the others at 1000) and scaled again when the sum passes the client cap. A blob shorter than 28 bytes or with a malformed value leaves the rates unchanged (D68). C# reads past a short field (`LLUDPClient.cs` lines 391–397; TB84) and a malformed value can stop queued traffic for every agent in the region (TB83). `wait` returns one hour for a bucket that is not finite. `tools/throttle-oracle` drives the pinned `LLUDPClient` constructor and `SetThrottles` and writes `fixtures/throttle/rates.json` (defaults, the bot's 6000 kb/s group, the floors, factor 0.5, ten clients on one scene, infinities, and the three D68 cases). `rates_match_the_csharp_oracle` matches those bit patterns and checks that the D68 cases keep the rates. `a_task_flood_stays_within_the_viewer_task_rate` advances one circuit by 0.5 s and keeps the task bytes inside the viewer rate times that window plus one packet. Resends, land and task wait on their buckets. A refused resend does not spend a retry. A packet skips the category queues only where C# sends it as Unknown: the handshake, `AgentMovementComplete`, chat, pings, acknowledgements, teleport start and failure, logout and disable. The arrival avatar `ObjectUpdate` (`LLClientView.cs` line 4788), `AvatarAppearance` (4656) and `AgentAlertMessage` (3243) are queued on Task. `LayerData` is encoded when it is queued, and `MapBlockReply` (2209 and 2238) is queued on Land; the hop is answered true once it is queued, as `SendMapBlock` only queues. The circuit clock is monotonic elapsed time. With more than 50 reliable packets awaiting acks only resends leave; land and task wait (`LLUDPClient.cs` lines 605–609). A child agent is not scaled by distance; the viewer packet uses factor 1. Child-agent throttles (the `AgentPosition` and `AgentData` throttles with distance scaling, and `GetThrottlesPacked` on the outgoing `AgentData`) are not this row; they are under Found while working. Queue-empty pulls, adaptive rate changes and message splitting stay out. TB83, TB84 and D68 are new. The 500-prim seeded-store run stays on P2.5a. `cargo xtask check` passed. `cargo xtask throttle verify` passed against `c401bceecd`. `cargo xtask live compare region` on 2026-11-25 from 03:51:15Z to 03:52:09Z, after the merge of `origin/main` at `c8cb594`, printed `region: match`, `login: pass`, `chat: pass`, `teleport: pass (40.0,200.0,30.0)`, `teleport neighbour: pass (1001,1000 and back)` and `live: run passed`. |
| P2.2a | DONE | P2.1a | `AgentUpdate` into movement intent. The significance filters (`LLClientView.cs` `CheckAgentMovementUpdateSignificance` 8783, camera 8808), `Source/OpenSim.Region.Framework/Scenes/ScenePresence.cs` `HandleAgentUpdate` (2737 to about 3100) and `AddNewMovement` (3977–4050), `SetAlwaysRun` (`LLClientView.cs` 9451), and the fly, sit-on-ground and stand flags, at `1d7abb8337`. Evidence: unit tests from the `AgentUpdate` records in `fixtures/captures/session.cap`. About a day. **Q44** (24 November 2026, `feat/agent-update`): rechecked at pin `c401bceecd`. Movement significance is `LLClientView.cs` lines 8783–8798, camera lines 8808–8819, and the packet handler lines 8822–8898 (both halves forced after 500 ms; a held control other than finish-anim is kept after 20 ms; a child, an in-transit avatar, or a sequence that is not newer is dropped). `ScenePresence.HandleAgentUpdate` is lines 2747–3018. `AddNewMovement` is lines 3987–4048. `SetAlwaysRun` is `LLClientView.cs` line 9451 and `ScenePresence.HandleSetAlwaysRun` lines 3953–3955. The frame stores the command: body and head rotation, control flags, draw distance, fly, sit-on-ground, stand, always-run, stop, the target velocity, and the fly roll. Walk and run speeds are 0.6 and 1.0 times 4.096; flying scales the command by four; a key release on the surface keeps the previous command for 250 ms; stop replaces it at 0.2 times 4.096. Sit on the ground zeros the command and ignores later direction until stand. A local teleport zeros the command and keeps fly, sit and always-run. The position step is P2.2b1. Avatars walk through prims; standing on prims is not possible. Read the pin's recent BasicPhysics edits (the carried-avatar comments) before porting. Stop at a border with no crossing. The stand-in is deleted in Phase 3. Harness: a `--physics basicphysics` C# region variant (`[Startup] physics = basicphysics`; the harness default is ubODE, `RegionServerDefaults.ini:313`), and a capture of a walk from it. Evidence: positions per tick match that capture within C#'s tolerances. **Q45** (24 November 2026, `feat/walk-integrator`): rechecked at pin `c401bceecd`. `Simulate` is lines 154–235 (the step is 159–231; line 234 returns the frame rate). `BasicPhysicsActor` is properties only. The carried-avatar comments are `BasicPhysicsScene.cs` lines 171–177 and `PhysicsScene.cs` lines 182–194. Nothing here sets that flag, so the branch is not taken. Each tick, for an avatar that is not sitting: XY by the physics velocity times 0.0909; a coordinate already outside is pulled to the 0.1 m inset and the velocity stays, and a step may finish outside. The presence tick then stops a leaving avatar: when the projection leaves the region, an axis already strictly outside is pushed by twice the velocity, and the physics velocity and the command become 0. A step that is still inside keeps its position. Ground is the integer cell of the old position, `y * width + x`, or 0 when there is no map. Standing Z is that cell plus 2.1 (the default box 1.9 plus the 0.2 adjust) and vertical velocity becomes 0. Flying adds vertical velocity unless the old Z plus that step is below the cell plus 2 m, in which case Z snaps to the same box and vertical velocity becomes 0. The next tick uses the velocity this step wrote. A key release moves on the deadline's tick, then the command and the physics velocity are 0. Login and teleport replies are the pose written in that call, before the next tick. The login pose is seated for chat immediately and the actor stays held until that arrival sample, so a tick cannot snap it first. Chat reads the pose after later ticks. The compare keeps X and Y on this run's arrival and accepts Z only when it is that arrival or the integer cell under it plus 2.1. A hop completion and a reused arrival zero the physics velocity and the command and clear the child flag. A child avatar is not integrated. Flying into the ground, when the last step was colliding and the command Z is negative, zeros the whole command. The animator still lands in a later row. Prims are ignored. The non-square stride is TB71 and D54: a region deeper than it is wide throws, and a region wider than it is deep reads the wrong cell. P2.0d only serves 256 by 256, so `live compare region` does not list it. `fixtures/walk/basicphysics-steps.json` is a BasicPhysics step trace from the pin-identical module (flat walk, fall, fly hold, fly snap, a start already outside, a sloped cell, and no map). Those rows, including the border overshoot, match `Simulate` bit for bit. `tools/walk-oracle` drives `BasicScene.Simulate` at the pin and checks the fixture; `fixtures/walk/README.md` says how. The live walk from a running `--physics basicphysics` region is validated by Q46a below. `a_leaving_step_rests_at_the_border` checks the presence stop. `cargo xtask live run <scenario> [grid] --physics basicphysics` writes `[Startup] physics = basicphysics` for each C# region. The default omits the key (ubODE, `RegionServerDefaults.ini` line 313). `live compare region` and `live up` reject the flag. Ferroan has no physics switch. A run that sets the flag fails unless the C# region log names basicphysics and does not show the ubODE library line. Terse updates and `walk: pass` are P2.2b2. `the_capture_matches_the_step_inside_the_region`, `a_leaving_step_rests_at_the_border`, `a_border_crossing_stops_the_body`, `flying_into_the_ground_zeros_the_command`, `a_child_is_not_integrated`, `a_return_rests_and_a_child_is_not_stepped`, `the_integer_cell_is_the_ground_under_the_old_position`, `a_tick_uses_the_physics_velocity_and_the_stop_lands_after_that_move`, `the_frame_ticks_and_stores_the_login_pose`, `basicphysics_is_opt_in_on_a_run`, `the_region_ini_has_the_const_keys_and_the_report_omits_the_password` and `the_region_ini_points_services_at_the_proxy_and_listens_elsewhere` passed. `cargo xtask check` passed. **Q46a** (24 November 2026, `feat/q46a-walk-fixture`): the live walk, at pin `c401bceecd`. `cargo xtask live run walk grid --physics basicphysics` (port base 23600) printed `walk: pass (128.0,128.0,26.0 to 134.0,128.3,26.9)` and `capture: 94 udp, 2 http, check passed, redact passed, replay passed`. `fixtures/walk/walk.cap` is the home region's redacted UDP and `fixtures/walk/terrain.bin` its stored heightmap (revision 23, `pinhead-island`). The frame queues a root's animation set, default first, for every open viewer, its own and children included, whenever its animator owes a pack (`SendAnimPack` through `ForEachScenePresence`: a new movement state, or a viewer's `AgentAnimation`), after each inbound post, answered call, avatar step and heartbeat; an arrival queues it for its own viewer and, after the appearance, for every other open viewer; `SendInitialData` queues each other root's set after its appearance. The circuit builds `AvatarAnimation` per viewer (`ferroan_client::avatar_animation`): the agent's own viewer gets the animations that name another object first, with those objects in `AnimationSourceList`, any other viewer gets the set in order with none, and `PhysicalAvatarEventList` is empty; reliable, unencoded, on the task outbox. The one-byte count always fits (`region.agent-animations`). `AgentAnimation` is checked for the circuit's ids and posted to the frame without an answer: each block starts (next sequence number, taken even when already playing or full; no object) or stops an animation (stopping the default resets it: another default becomes `STAND` 1, a `STAND` default keeps its sequence), a nil id changes nothing, and one pack follows whatever changed; a child (away or not yet arrived) changes nothing and sends nothing. **Evidence:** `fixtures/animations/animations.cap`, a new C# `live run animations grid --physics basicphysics` (run `27040-2939964`, checked, replayed and redacted; README there); `the_csharp_walk_animation_packets_are_built_byte_for_byte` (each of `fixtures/walk/walk.cap`'s three C# packets, re-encoded first, rebuilt from its set and framed with its header, sequence and acks, is the datagram byte for byte); `its_own_viewer_gets_the_object_animations_first_with_their_objects`; `a_viewer_starts_and_stops_animations_with_one_pack_per_packet` (with the stop of a `STAND` default) and `a_start_past_the_bound_takes_a_number_and_adds_nothing` (`animation::tests`); `a_walk_and_its_stop_reach_every_open_viewer_children_included`, `a_viewer_animation_packet_sends_one_pack_and_a_child_is_refused` and the arrival and `SendInitialData` orders, a child viewer at the arrival included, in `an_arrival_is_announced_and_the_newcomer_hears_of_the_others` and `a_viewer_not_yet_open_hears_of_the_others_once_at_its_initial_data` (`frame::tests`); `an_agent_animation_is_forwarded_block_by_block` and `avatar_packets_leave_in_order_before_the_terse_blocks` (circuit); `the_scene_feed_writes_another_avatars_packets` and `the_scene_feed_gives_only_the_agents_own_viewer_the_object_layout` (region; a set naming another object, queued on the feed, gets the object-first layout only on the agent's own viewer). New scenario `animations` (one `SCENARIOS` row): the owner walks, stops, then starts and stops an animation with `AgentAnimation`; the watcher must see exactly `STAND` 1, `WALK` n, `STAND` n + 1, the started animation at n + 2 after the default, and its stop; on Ferroan the owner's packets (re-encode check first) must match the C# capture byte for byte, as runs of two (one per viewer): the body byte for byte and, of the header, the first byte and the extra-header length, with every animation sequence number but 1 moved by one offset for the whole capture (`xtask/src/animations_capture.rs`, `the_csharp_capture_has_the_five_steps_to_both_viewers`). Both runs are in `live.yml`. Local runs (port base 27040): `live run animations grid --region ferroan` “animations: pass (the observer saw STAND 1, WALK 5410350, STAND 5410351, the started animation at 5410352 and its stop)” and “animation bytes: 10 AvatarAnimation for the walker … each run [2, 2, 2, 2, 2] long) match the C# capture byte for byte” (run `27040-3044404`); `animations grid --physics basicphysics` “animations: pass (… WALK 5420582, STAND 5420583 …)” (run `27040-3061658`); `walk grid --region ferroan` “walk: pass (forward movement, observer kills=1, leaving viewer kills=0)” (run `27040-3065366`); `avatars grid --region ferroan` “avatars: pass” with the avatar bytes line (run `27040-3070593`); `neighbour grid --region ferroan` “teleport neighbour: pass (1001,1000 and back)” (run `27040-3082731`). `cargo xtask check` passed. Parcel-hidden avatars, an object-started animation's capture and the dropped refused post are in `docs/found/Q50.md`. Firestorm remains pending. |

Ferroan does not answer the request, so Ferrobot's `nearby` waits about 10 s per call for names that never come. At Pin8 `c401bceecd`, `LLClientView.cs` `HandleUUIDNameRequest` (10806–10819) ignores the request when the presence is missing, deleted or in transit (other than a local teleport) and raises one lookup per block. `UserManagementModule.cs` `HandleUUIDNameRequest` (`Source/OpenSim.Region.CoreModules/Framework/UserManagement/UserManagementModule.cs` 204–237) answers at once from its user cache when the entry has `HasGridUserTried`; otherwise it queues the lookup on the service throttle (category `uuidname`) and answers when `GetUser` (995–1082) finds the user: the account service's `getaccount` (first and last name), then GridUser's `getgriduserinfo` UUI (a local home gives first and last; a foreign one gives `first.last` and `@host:port`). An unknown id gets no reply. A looked-up entry stays cached 1,800,000 ms. `SendNameReply` (`LLClientView.cs` 8443–8456) sends one `UUIDNameBlock` per reply on the task throttle, names through `Util.StringToBytes256`. The region already has the `getaccount` and `getgriduserinfo` clients (`crates/ferroan-region/src/grid.rs`). Bound the blocks per request, the waiting lookups and the cache, each with a limit id in docs/LIMITS.md; the lookup never blocks the circuit or the frame thread. **Evidence:** a `ferroan-bot` step asks the C# region for another logged-in avatar's name and for an unknown id; the captured `UUIDNameReply` body is a fixture, Ferroan's reply matches it byte for byte (names redacted the same way), and the unknown id gets no reply on either; `cargo xtask live run ferrobot grid --region ferroan` shows `nearby` answering without the 10 s wait, with its timing before and after in the PR. Size: about a day. **Q84** (25 November 2026, `feat/q84-uuid-names`): rechecked at `c401bceecd`, with `ServiceThrottleModule.Enqueue` and `JobEngine` (5,000 jobs, two at a time) and `LLClientView.SendNameReply` (task category, reliable, unencoded, one block per reply, no batching). `ferroan-client`'s `RegionNames` holds the cache and the region queue; the circuit answers a cached id at once and queues the rest; `ferroan-region`'s `GridNames` reuses the `getaccount` and `getgriduserinfo` clients and ports `ParseFullUniversalUserIdentifier`, `CheckUrl` and `IsLocalGrid` (gatekeeper settings as `GridInfo` reads them, without DNS). An arrival seeds its names as `CacheUserName` does. A cached miss is answered `Unknown` and the id on later requests, as C# does (TB89, D73). Bounds: `region.name-lookups` (5,000), `region.name-circuit-lookups` (512), `region.name-cache` (8,192), `region.name-replies` (1,024); a request carries at most 255 ids on the wire. Evidence: `names::tests` (cache, unknown id, arrival, GridUser, bounds, expiry, reply layout and 255-byte names; the unknown-id and circuit-bound tests fail when those rules are removed), `a_name_request_answers_the_known_id_only` (circuit), `names` tests in `ferroan-region` (identifier parsing and locality), `avatars_capture` tests. The `avatars` scenario now has each bot ask for the other and for an unknown id, then the unknown id again; C# `live run avatars grid` passes and is `fixtures/avatars/names.cap` (the redactor keeps the unknown id's `Unknown` and id, which name nobody); `live run avatars grid --region ferroan` passes and prints `3 UUIDNameReply (each bot's name, and the unknown id's cached miss) match it byte for byte`. `live run ferrobot grid --region ferroan`: `NearmeWithDetails took 10003 ms` before, `3 ms` after (one local run each). |

| P2.11a3a | DONE (this PR) | P2.11a1, P2.11a2 | One owner for each child destination of a root circuit generation (Q79c1). The seed is stored before the admission POST, as `EnableChildAgents` does (`EntityTransferModule.cs` 2410–2411 at Pin8 `c401bceecd`). A second claim of that destination does not start another POST. Refusal drops the claim and sends no viewer events. Logout during the POST closes an accepted child once and does not announce it (TB148, [D106](docs/DECISIONS.md)). A completion for an older generation does not announce a different claim on the replacement, and it does not close a destination that is still the remote root. The same session carries a hop-promoted neighbour with that flag still set, and that completion announces once. A failed POST before logout drops the claim. Queued closes of a replaced root are sent from a separate task (`close_staged_returns_while_the_neighbour_holds_the_close`), so the new login does not wait on them. An in-view hop reuses the stored caps path (`GetChildSeed`, 1060–1061); a destination outside the view gets a new path. The visibility circuit takes that same path. Past 64 destinations for one generation, admission refuses and reports `region.child-destinations`. Evidence: `children` tests cover one owner, refusal, logout during admission, a stale completion, the bound and LLSD equality with [fixtures/child-view/events.xml](fixtures/child-view/events.xml), including the captured seed joined from `trace.json`. A same-session carry keeps a hop-promoted neighbour promoted until release, and a later session does not stage a close of that root. `fail_child_then_claim_child_returns_new` drops a failed POST so the next claim is new; a failure after logout still closes once. `a_carried_promoted_claim_announces_once` announces after that carry. Queued closes run on a separate task (`close_staged_returns_while_the_neighbour_holds_the_close`). `an_in_view_hop_reuses_the_admission_seed` checks the child POST `caps_path` and `TeleportFinish` `SeedCapability`. `a_visible_child_posts_the_claimed_seed` posts Q79b's child circuit with the claimed path. Live `neighbour grid` on ferroan, port base 28128, run `28128-3155650`: `CloseAgent 1` on the neighbour and `teleport neighbour: pass`. Q79c2 drives the neighbour set from the frame. |
| P2.11a3b1 | DONE (this PR) | P2.11a3a | Finish every child claim exactly once, and close children on a far hop (Q79c2a). The claim guard is returned only for a new claim. Dropping it follows the failure path: before logout the claim is dropped, and after logout or on a stale generation one close is staged with the claim's session ([D106](docs/DECISIONS.md)). A timeout and a transport failure follow that same path. The caller POSTs, waits 500 ms, then reports; viewer events follow only an announce (`EntityTransferModule.cs` 2780–2786 at Pin8 `c401bceecd`). A lease drop forgets open children and stages nothing. A far hop stages a close only for an open child outside the destination's view, or every known child when the destination's grid Y is 0, and never the destination itself. Evidence: `a_dropped_claim_can_be_claimed_again`, `a_guard_is_not_issued_for_a_claim_another_task_owns`, `a_settled_claim_keeps_the_open_child`, `aborting_an_admission_post_drops_the_claim_until_logout`, `a_timeout_after_logout_closes_with_the_claim_session`, `a_stale_close_uses_the_session_that_claimed_it`, `pause_before_announce_waits_half_a_second`, `a_dropped_lease_forgets_open_children_and_stages_nothing`, `a_far_hop_closes_a_farther_child_and_forgets_an_adjacent_one`, `a_child_circuit_drop_after_an_in_view_hop_stages_nothing`, `a_far_hop_deletes_an_open_child_with_the_claim_session`, and `close_staged_returns_while_the_neighbour_holds_the_close`. `cargo xtask check` passed. Q79c2b drives the neighbour set from the frame. |
| P2.11a3b2 | DONE (this PR) | P2.11a3b1 | Drive the neighbour set from the frame (Q79c2b). On arrival a root arms one enable from `SendInitialData` (`ScenePresence.cs` 4395–4407 at Pin8 `c401bceecd`; 2573–2584 is only a border crossing) and on the child half of `CheckForSignificantMovement` (4750–4828), diff the visible neighbours. The 16 m movement trigger does not enable children. A new neighbour goes through the admission task. A neighbour that leaves is closed with the session from `Admission::Close`. A neighbour that stays gets `AgentPosition` on the 2 s gate and the 10 m / 32 m tests. The scene thread does not wait on HTTP. Evidence: `child_gate_follows_the_pin8_child_half`, `send_initial_data_arms_one_enable_per_arrival`, `arrival_offers_enable_without_blocking_when_the_channel_is_full`, `leave_view_closes_an_open_child_and_holds_a_pending_one`, `frame_drive_admits_updates_and_closes_a_visible_neighbour`, `a_teleport_root_posts_children_after_it_is_marked_root`, `position_pass_sends_one_update_per_server_uri`, `a_cancelled_admission_is_taken_at_serve_end`, and `ferroan_child_view_matches_the_capture_except_time_and_throttles`. Arrival stores `EnvironmentTickCount()+10000`; `EnvironmentTickCountSubtract` (`Util.cs` 3620–3623) wraps that future stamp, so the 2 s gate does not hold on it (TB155, D113). ## Phase 3: Physics

Gate: vehicle, walking and collision tests give bit-identical traces to the C# Jolt module when the calls match ([D144](docs/DECISIONS.md)). The maintainer deferred the C# module move on 26 November 2026 and reversed the deferral the same day. The move landed as `dev-build` `41f18f983b` (OpenSim-NGC #550, P3.0c), Pin9 names it (P3.0d) and D144 records the choice (P3.0e). The full oracle P3.0 can start. The cross-platform gate P3.28 waits on its other rows; on the C# side it is linux-x64 only until P3.0g records a deterministic win-x64 joltc. P3.29 is blocked because it is split into P3.29a and P3.29b. P3.13a is blocked because it is split into P3.13a1 and P3.13a2. P3.13a2 waits on the character bind (P3.13b). P3.1, P3.1b, P3.29a and P3.13a1 are done. Every other row proceeds. It closes on a Pin8 compile, a stock recording (P3.57), or the published envelope in [the Phase 3 plan](docs/plans/PHASE3-PHYSICS.md). The exact native trace named in a proceeding row does not have to pass for that row to close.

## Phase 4: Scripting

Gate: Linden's conformance suite at the pinned `slua`, plus the Phlox tests and the corpus, on a Rust region. Stage 1 is the engine. The pure-function plan and the communication plan are the stage 2 rows for those families. A placeholder those plans replace does not start. The other placeholders stay blocked until their plan exists. The evidence for stage 1 is the stage 1 cell in [the Phase 4 plan](docs/plans/PHASE4-SCRIPTING.md). Queue Q230 to Q376.

| P4.29 | TODO | P4.1c | **Q294.** Refuse a script only YEngine can compile. About a day. Runnable now. It does not wait on the C# LLSD spec. A script is YEngine-only when Pin8 would route it to YEngine and `slua`'s compile refuses it. `ResolveScriptEngine` (`Scene.Inventory.cs` 108–202) routes to YEngine in four places: a saved engine choice of YEngine when that engine is loaded (135–142), a `//YEngine:` header when that engine is loaded (146–164), saved YEngine state (step 4, 177–188), and a YEngine default engine (step 6, 201). Step 1 does not select YEngine, because YEngine does not implement `AcceptsCompileTarget`. A choice or header for an engine that is not loaded falls through and does not count. A `yoption` or `yoptions;` body is not a routing signal. On a Phlox-default region that body, with no header, no saved choice and no saved YEngine state, reaches Phlox at step 6. The tokenizer match is case-insensitive (`strcasecmp`, `MMRScriptTokenize.cs` 612 and 622). Any other compile failure is an ordinary error. The report calls `log_boundary` with route `script.yengine-only`, naming the object, the item and the reason. The route is not a catalog id. `refuse` and `drop_bound` take counts, and a catalog id needs a [LIMITS.md](docs/LIMITS.md) entry, so this report adds no LIMITS.md row. The script is not started. The operator keeps that object on a C# region until it is ported. C# sources are in [the Phase 4 plan](docs/plans/PHASE4-SCRIPTING.md). |

## Phase 1: Grid services

Gate: each Rust service runs behind a C# grid in the live harness. A C# region using it passes the same scenarios as with the C# service. The C# service reads what the Rust service wrote, and the Rust service reads what the C# service wrote, on the same database and files, so an operator can switch back at any time. The scope is in the [plan](docs/PLAN.md).

The three asset backends are those Tranquillity ships (18 November 2026, `8583218`): `AssetService` on SQLite, MySQL and PostgreSQL (`AssetStore`); `XAssetService` on MySQL and PostgreSQL (`XAssetStore`); and FSAssets on MySQL and PostgreSQL plus a file tree (`FSAssetStore`). `ferroan-robust` serves all three behind the same asset HTTP routes, chosen by `[AssetService] LocalServiceModule` as in C#.

Every Phase 1 row, besides its own acceptance:

- Route by route, the HTTP request and reply forms match fixtures captured from the C# service at the pin (dates and generated ids folded).
- Data access works on each database Tranquillity supports for that service (SQLite, MySQL, PostgreSQL), on tables its own migrations created. The C# and Rust services read each other's rows.
- In grid mode with that service served by `ferroan-robust` (P1.0), every harness scenario passes, and `cargo xtask live compare <service>` matches the C# service's normalized HTTP.
- A `ferroan-bot` scenario or a Ferrobot check exercises the service from a client wherever a client can reach it.
- Size it from the C# before starting. If it is more than two weeks of work, set it to `BLOCKED` with a proposed split.

| ID | Status | Depends on | Task and acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| P1.A1 | DONE | P0.9 | `AssetService` on every database Tranquillity supports. Add MySQL and PostgreSQL to `ferroan-services`' asset store, following `Source/OpenSim.Data.MySQL/MySQLAssetData.cs` and `Source/OpenSim.Data.PGSQL/PGSQLAssetData.cs` at the pin, including their access-time updates and how each one handles a store of an id that already exists. Close the gaps the P0.9 pilot left (docs/PLAN.md, "Asset pilot measurement"): updating an existing asset where C# allows it, `FetchAssetMetadataSet`, the connector's console commands (`show asset`, `dump asset`, `delete asset`), and the default asset loader (`DefaultAssetLoader`, `AssetLoaderArgs`, `OpenSim.Framework.AssetLoader.Filesystem`), so `ferroan-robust` seeds the library itself instead of borrowing C# GridServer. The ordinary CI job gains MySQL and PostgreSQL service containers with throwaway credentials, no secrets, and sets `FERROAN_MYSQL_URL` and `FERROAN_POSTGRES_URL`. Acceptance: store, fetch, metadata, exists and delete fixtures pass on all three databases. `cargo xtask live assets --db sqlite\|mysql\|pgsql` prints `assets: match` for each. The C# service reads assets the Rust service wrote, and the reverse, on each database. Also resolve TB1 (found in the Q10b audit): `crates/ferroan-services/src/asset/xml.rs::flags_text` writes decimal text for flag bits outside 1, 2 and 4, where C#'s `XmlSerializer` throws. Capture what the C# asset route returns for such a stored asset, match it, and record the decision in docs/TRANQUILLITY_BUGS.md. Offered to outside contributors on 19 November 2026, split into [#59](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/issues/59) (MySQL and PostgreSQL stores, `FetchAssetMetadataSet`, CI database containers), [#60](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/issues/60) (updating assets, the default asset loader, console command logic) and [#61](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/issues/61) (TB1). The manager marks this row `DONE` when all three have merged. **#59** merged as PR [#127](https://github.com/KanikaBreed/Ferroan/pull/127): the asset store speaks SQLite (AssetStore 6), MySQL (10) and PostgreSQL (9), following `MySQLAssetData.cs` and `PGSQLAssetData.cs` at `c70fee205f`. `fixtures/db/mysql` and `fixtures/db/postgres` are `Migration.Update` dumps (`cargo xtask schema servers`). Store, fetch, metadata, exists and delete run on all three when `FERROAN_MYSQL_URL` and `FERROAN_POSTGRES_URL` are set; ordinary Linux CI loads those dumps. `cargo xtask asset cross` is the C# plugin reading Rust rows and the reverse. The harness `--db mysql|pgsql` switch stays refused. **#60:** `ferroan-robust` seeds the library when `DefaultAssetLoader` names `OpenSim.Framework.AssetLoader.Filesystem.dll` and `AssetLoaderArgs` is an `AssetSets.xml` path. A repeated section name keeps the first section. A duplicate key, a bad id, a bad type, or a missing named file fails the load before any store. The loader callback rewrites a row only when the SHA1 of the bytes differs. `show_asset`, `dump_asset` and `delete_asset` match `AssetServerConnector`'s text and are not wired to the remote console. `cargo xtask live run assets` seeds through `ferroan-robust`. HTTP `POST /assets/{id}` still returns false. `Migration.Update` is still unimplemented. **#61:** at `c70fee205f`, `StoredAssetFlags.FromStored` masks a stored 8 to `known_flags` 0 and a stored 9 (Maptile mixed with bit 8) to `known_flags` 1. `XmlSerializer` accepts both (`threw` false). `fixtures/asset/asset/unnamed-flags.xml` and `fixtures/asset/asset/mixed-flags.xml` are the `AssetServerGetHandler` bodies (`<Flags>Normal</Flags>` and `<Flags>Maptile</Flags>`). `GET /assets/{id}` returns those bytes. ## Asset system

The new asset backend ([ASSET_SYSTEM.md](docs/ASSET_SYSTEM.md), D23 to D28). It is served by `ferroan-robust` behind the asset routes of P1.A1 to P1.A3, so the Phase 1 rules above apply to it too, except that its storage is its own: switching back is by sync and export (D27). Planned against Tranquillity with every open OpenSim-NGC PR merged (D21). Size each row from the design before starting; a row over two weeks is set to `BLOCKED` with a proposed split.

| ID | Status | Depends on | Task and acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| AS0 | TODO | P1.0 | Fault probes in the live harness. Port the FSAssets fault probe to `cargo xtask live` (cut-short, empty and removed files, a name with a four-byte character, the database paused during a store, a full disk on a small tmpfs) and run Ferrobot's `hg-content` scenario on a second grid. Against the C# backends each case records the TB22 to TB26 behaviour as a known-difference test (P0.10n4); against the new backend each must pass. |
| AS1 | TODO | P1.A1, AS0 | `ferroan-assets` core: the catalog on SQLite and MySQL/MariaDB with the startup check (D23), the storage-plugin interface, the local-files plugin, the store and read paths of ASSET_SYSTEM.md §5 (durable before answering, hash checked on every read, existence only for healthy assets), the outbox, the local read cache, and the asset routes with C#'s forms. Acceptance: every AS0 case passes; `cargo xtask live compare assets --backend ferroan` matches C# on the routes; a loop that kills the process during stores leaves no row without its bytes. |

CI runs SeaweedFS in a container as it runs MySQL. Acceptance: AS1's checks on SeaweedFS; a volume server stopped mid-store gives a failure or an outbox entry, never a lost asset. |
| AS3 | TODO | AS1 | S3 plugin, with small assets packed into larger objects. A pure-Rust client (D8). Acceptance: AS1's checks against SeaweedFS's S3 gateway and Garage in CI. |

Acceptance: on a generated store with planted damage, the report counts each class exactly; a second run changes nothing; an orphan whose bytes are in a cache file comes back under its id. |

Acceptance: a damaged blob is found by the scrubber, repaired from a matching copy, and its history shows each step. |

Phases 5 to 7 get the rest of their rows when they near their start. Their scope is in the [plan](docs/PLAN.md).

Pin3 carry-forward for future land and estate rows (Phase 5 in PLAN.md; no such rows exist here yet): preserve #422/#423/#425/#426's parcel-only flood masks, BlockTerraform and AllowLandJoinDivide authorization, empty-subdivision refusal, absent UDP parcel-field preservation, finite in-parcel landing points and reset-before-save, missing-cell overlay progress, non-square bounds, unlocked event dispatch, permission-before-recount, and persistence of field-only role edits. Terrain transfer must bound and sequence uploads, re-ACK duplicates, release idle/disconnected uploads, and download per request in memory. These are source notes, not new task scope; [the complete commit review](docs/PIN3_REVIEW.md) identifies their implementations.
